# Architecture Decision Records atomic clause inventory

## Pinned source and evidence boundary

This is a clause-level extraction of the fetched-text snapshot for the canonical [Architecture Decision Records](https://docs.google.com/document/d/1K6JwlbSQIRvxmvSeTb5oNEQYc5SfoIJ4QPVTGtXPCbo/edit?usp=drivesdk), not a native Google Docs export. The text digest is over the captured UTF-8 bytes exactly, including a leading BOM; it does not claim byte identity with the native document. Logical line numbers below are 1-based after splitting the captured CRLF text, with the BOM retained only in the byte digest. Every logical source line is represented by one or more atoms or by an explicit disposition in the coverage index.

The [current-source inventory](control-plane-source-inventory.md) retains cross-source provenance, and the [machine requirement ledger](control-plane-requirements.v1.json) remains unchanged by this clause extraction. The source-line maps below do not upgrade requirement classifications or candidate acceptance evidence.

This is a bounded source extraction plus repository-path mapping. A named Control Plane path means the file was located, not that a test ran or a behavior passed. Product ownership outside this repository stays with the named external owner. The extraction does not update requirement classifications, candidate evidence, issue state, or acceptance verdicts. It does not claim that a cited document or a product has been fully accepted.

List members extracted from a line retain the governing line through that reference. “Current decision” means the source record is not marked superseded; it is not a verification or acceptance statement.

| `ADR-L101-04` | ADR-014: Desktop-First Client and Local-First Runtime Architecture, L101                                    | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Agent HQ-managed cloud runtime infrastructure is a later product expansion aimed primarily at consumer and unattended 24/7 workforce use rather than an MVP dependency.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-DEPLOY`, `CP-RUNTIME`                                               |

| `ADR-L104-02` | ADR-014: Desktop-First Client and Local-First Runtime Architecture, L104                                    | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | The desktop app contains the full workspace UI plus a local Runtime Host                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-DEPLOY`, `CP-RUNTIME`                                               |
| `ADR-L104-03` | ADR-014: Desktop-First Client and Local-First Runtime Architecture, L104                                    | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Web/mobile reuse the synchronized product model but do not become local execution environments.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-DEPLOY`, `CP-RUNTIME`                                               |

| `ADR-L104-06` | ADR-014: Desktop-First Client and Local-First Runtime Architecture, L104                                    | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Offline local nodes make their runtimes unavailable and work may be queued or reported unavailable.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-DEPLOY`, `CP-RUNTIME`                                               |
| `ADR-L104-07` | ADR-014: Desktop-First Client and Local-First Runtime Architecture, L104                                    | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | After Agent HQ Cloud execution exists, fallback to it may occur only under an explicit user-enabled policy and never as a silent or default migration.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-DEPLOY`, `CP-RUNTIME`                                               |
| `ADR-L104-08` | ADR-014: Desktop-First Client and Local-First Runtime Architecture, L104                                    | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Managed Pi in the MVP primarily means Agent HQ-managed Pi running on the user's local desktop node.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-DEPLOY`, `CP-RUNTIME`                                               |
| `ADR-L104-09` | ADR-014: Desktop-First Client and Local-First Runtime Architecture, L104                                    | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Later Agent HQ Cloud Runtime nodes should use isolated, suspendable/ephemeral compute with persistent workspace/runtime state where appropriate rather than assuming one always-on VPS per user.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-DEPLOY`, `CP-RUNTIME`                                               |
| `ADR-L104-10` | ADR-014: Desktop-First Client and Local-First Runtime Architecture, L104                                    | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | ADR-014 supersedes prior wording that treated the web client as the primary product surface or the local bridge as an optional edge integration.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-DEPLOY`, `CP-RUNTIME`                                               |
| `ADR-L108-01` | ADR-015: HTTP Commands + SSE for Real-Time Event Delivery, L108                                             | Current decision                                                                                                                                         | Use standard HTTP for client commands and Server-Sent Events (SSE) for ordered server-to-client real-time product events.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-EVENTS`                                                             |
| `ADR-L108-02` | ADR-015: HTTP Commands + SSE for Real-Time Event Delivery, L108                                             | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Persist durable WorkspaceEvents before delivery and support cursor-based replay after reconnect.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-EVENTS`                                                             |

| `ADR-L131-01` | ADR-018: Idempotent Cross-Service Execution and Reconciliation, L131                                        | Current decision                                                                                                                                         | Agent HQ and the Control Plane treat state-changing cross-service work as an at-least-once distributed workflow, not a shared transaction.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `CP-WORKFLOW`, `CP-API`, `CP-EVENTS`, `EXT-HQ`                                    |
| `ADR-L131-02` | ADR-018: Idempotent Cross-Service Execution and Reconciliation, L131                                        | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Agent HQ persists product intent plus a CommandOutbox record before delivery.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `CP-WORKFLOW`, `CP-API`, `CP-EVENTS`, `EXT-HQ`                                    |
| `ADR-L131-03` | ADR-018: Idempotent Cross-Service Execution and Reconciliation, L131                                        | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Every command carries stable request_id, workspace/operation-scoped idempotency_key, canonical payload hash, and correlation metadata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `CP-WORKFLOW`, `CP-API`, `CP-EVENTS`, `EXT-HQ`                                    |
| `ADR-L131-04` | ADR-018: Idempotent Cross-Service Execution and Reconciliation, L131                                        | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | The Control Plane records commands in CommandInbox and guarantees identical retries resolve to one logical execution_id while conflicting key reuse fails closed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 | `CP-WORKFLOW`, `CP-API`, `CP-EVENTS`, `EXT-HQ`                                    |
| `ADR-L131-05` | ADR-018: Idempotent Cross-Service Execution and Reconciliation, L131                                        | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Agent HQ persists normalized Control Plane events in an EventInbox before applying them to product state and appending redacted WorkspaceEvents.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `CP-WORKFLOW`, `CP-API`, `CP-EVENTS`, `EXT-HQ`                                    |
| `ADR-L134-01` | ADR-018: Idempotent Cross-Service Execution and Reconciliation, L134                                        | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | One logical execution keeps one execution_id                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `CP-WORKFLOW`, `CP-API`, `CP-EVENTS`, `EXT-HQ`                                    |
| `ADR-L134-02` | ADR-018: Idempotent Cross-Service Execution and Reconciliation, L134                                        | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Concrete retries use distinct attempt_id values.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `CP-WORKFLOW`, `CP-API`, `CP-EVENTS`, `EXT-HQ`                                    |
| `ADR-L134-03` | ADR-018: Idempotent Cross-Service Execution and Reconciliation, L134                                        | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Unknown or ambiguous outcomes enter reconciliation_required rather than being guessed.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `CP-WORKFLOW`, `CP-API`, `CP-EVENTS`, `EXT-HQ`                                    |
| `ADR-L134-04` | ADR-018: Idempotent Cross-Service Execution and Reconciliation, L134                                        | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Periodic/on-demand reconciliation compares durable state with authoritative read/status APIs.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `CP-WORKFLOW`, `CP-API`, `CP-EVENTS`, `EXT-HQ`                                    |
| `ADR-L134-05` | ADR-018: Idempotent Cross-Service Execution and Reconciliation, L134                                        | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Downstream effect-producing operations reuse idempotency keys when supported, reconcile against authoritative status when available, and are not blindly retried when a non-idempotent outcome is ambiguous.                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `CP-WORKFLOW`, `CP-API`, `CP-EVENTS`, `EXT-HQ`                                    |

| `ADR-L138-04` | ADR-019: Provider-Independent Agent HQ Principal Identity, L138                                             | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | RuntimeNode devices, internal services, persistent Agents, and Workers remain distinct principal types with separately scoped credentials and permissions.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |

| `ADR-L155-02` | ADR-021: Desktop Client Packaging and Authentication Model, L155                                            | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Callback state, nonce, PKCE verifier, audience, app-instance binding, and one-time-code expiry/replay protections are required.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L155-03` | ADR-021: Desktop Client Packaging and Authentication Model, L155                                            | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Sign-out/session revocation is distinct from RuntimeNode unpairing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L155-04` | ADR-021: Desktop Client Packaging and Authentication Model, L155                                            | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Offline behavior is explicit: cached UI may render, but mutations/execution require a valid cloud session unless a later ADR defines offline queuing.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |

| `ADR-L166-09` | ADR-023: Artifact Metadata and Storage Boundary, L166                                                       | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | The Control Plane may use the same Cloudflare account/provider through its independently owned S3-compatible ObjectStore boundary for Control Plane-owned bundles, eval data, and durable execution objects, but the two products use separate buckets or environment-isolated bucket sets and independently scoped credentials.                                                                                                  | `CP-ARTIFACT`                                                                     |

| `ADR-L166-12` | ADR-023: Artifact Metadata and Storage Boundary, L166                                                       | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Domain/API/client contracts remain provider-neutral and reference stable artifact_id values plus opaque storage metadata.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-ARTIFACT`                                                           |

| `ADR-L169-01` | ADR-023: Artifact Metadata and Storage Boundary, L169                                                       | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | R2 buckets remain private and are accessed through narrowly scoped product/environment credentials or short-lived single-object capabilities.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-ARTIFACT`                                                           |
| `ADR-L169-02` | ADR-023: Artifact Metadata and Storage Boundary, L169                                                       | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Agent HQ and Control Plane storage credentials are independently scoped even when the Cloudflare account/provider is shared.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-ARTIFACT`                                                           |

| `ADR-L169-04` | ADR-023: Artifact Metadata and Storage Boundary, L169                                                       | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | An explicitly enabled multipart large-Artifact lane is bounded at 1 GiB.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-ARTIFACT`                                                           |
| `ADR-L169-05` | ADR-023: Artifact Metadata and Storage Boundary, L169                                                       | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Upload capabilities expire after 15 minutes and download capabilities after 5 minutes.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-ARTIFACT`                                                           |
| `ADR-L169-06` | ADR-023: Artifact Metadata and Storage Boundary, L169                                                       | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Promoted bytes remain pending/unavailable until final size, detected media type, SHA-256 digest, ownership, file-class policy, and malware verification pass.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-ARTIFACT`                                                           |
| `ADR-L169-07` | ADR-023: Artifact Metadata and Storage Boundary, L169                                                       | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Malware scanning sits behind a replaceable MalwareScanner port                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-ARTIFACT`                                                           |
| `ADR-L169-08` | ADR-023: Artifact Metadata and Storage Boundary, L169                                                       | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | The initial first-party implementation uses an isolated ClamAV/clamd Railway worker with at least daily signature refresh and fail-closed scanner health.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-ARTIFACT`                                                           |
| `ADR-L169-09` | ADR-023: Artifact Metadata and Storage Boundary, L169                                                       | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Positive, unscannable, encrypted/password-protected archive, timeout, scanner-unavailable, or unhealthy-signature outcomes cannot produce an available Artifact.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-ARTIFACT`                                                           |
| `ADR-L169-10` | ADR-023: Artifact Metadata and Storage Boundary, L169                                                       | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Large transfers go directly to/from R2 rather than ordinary request handlers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-ARTIFACT`                                                           |
| `ADR-L169-11` | ADR-023: Artifact Metadata and Storage Boundary, L169                                                       | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | The S3-compatible and MalwareScanner boundaries preserve provider replacement without changing Artifact identity/lifecycle semantics.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-ARTIFACT`                                                           |
| `ADR-L169-12` | ADR-023: Artifact Metadata and Storage Boundary, L169                                                       | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Local-only Artifacts become unavailable when their owning RuntimeNode/grant is unavailable.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       | `EXT-HQ`, `CP-ARTIFACT`                                                           |

| `ADR-L173-01` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L173                                               | Current decision                                                                                                                                         | Agent HQ Neon PostgreSQL WorkspaceEvents remain the source of truth for product event delivery.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L173-02` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L173                                               | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Each workspace has a monotonically increasing event sequence.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                     | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L173-03` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L173                                               | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | The initial long-lived Event Gateway runs on Railway and authorizes subscriptions, replays retained WorkspaceEvents from Neon, then streams committed events over SSE.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                            | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L173-04` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L173                                               | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Cursor v1 is a server-issued opaque base64url value binding cursor version, workspace ID, last workspace_sequence, and last event ID and authenticated with HMAC-SHA256 under a rotating server-only cursor key.                                                                                                                                                                                                                  | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L173-05` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L173                                               | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | PostgreSQL LISTEN/NOTIFY is the preferred wake-up path on a direct/session-capable connection, but notifications carry only event identity/sequence and are advisory                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L173-06` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L173                                               | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | A bounded database catch-up/polling path preserves correctness when notifications are missed or unavailable.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L176-01` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L176                                               | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | The initial online replay window is at least 30 days                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L176-02` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L176                                               | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | A cursor outside that window or whose continuity cannot be proven returns explicit resync_required.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L176-03` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L176                                               | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | SSE heartbeats are emitted every 15 seconds, replay pages are bounded at 500 events, and notification failure falls back to 2-second bounded Neon polling until LISTEN/NOTIFY recovers.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                           | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L176-04` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L176                                               | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Default connection limits are five concurrent streams per user/workspace and twenty per authenticated user                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L176-05` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L176                                               | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Clients reconnect with jittered backoff and any gateway replica can resume from the signed cursor.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L176-06` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L176                                               | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Gateway shutdown stops new streams and allows up to 30 seconds for bounded replay/handoff cleanup.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L176-07` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L176                                               | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Railway instances remain disposable because Neon is authoritative.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |
| `ADR-L176-08` | ADR-024: Scalable SSE Gateway and WorkspaceEvent Replay, L176                                               | Current consequence / implementation obligation                                                                                                          | Kafka/Redis Streams or another broker is introduced only if measured throughput/operational requirements justify it.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              | `EXT-HQ`                                                                          |

## Acceptance boundary

This inventory is source evidence and a bounded map of reachable Control Plane files. It is not an implementation verification, test run, packaged-product check, cross-product integration result, human review, or candidate acceptance. For Agent HQ and Cortana-owned clauses, no external repository code or evidence is asserted. For superseded ADRs, the inventory preserves the historical decision without reviving it. Existing machine-ledger classifications and candidate evidence remain unchanged.
